- ~~**P3 · M5 needs a third category: SCROLL-BY-DESIGN (opened HO 615, filed NOT taken)**~~ — **TAKEN AT HO 620 as instrument v4's `M5s` column** (`59a45cd`, corrected `62c25e4`). Shipped exactly where this entry said it belonged: an instrument commit with its own falsification leg, and the fixture carrying its positive and its negative. **One DEFINITION DEVIATION from the shape below, stated because it is a definition and not a detail:** the literal *"…with an animated or explicitly-scrollable **CHILD**"* misses `pre.bdp-rawpre` — **one of this entry's own two worked examples** — because a `<pre>`'s only child is a text node. v4's clause (a) reads the **element itself** as the scrollable thing (own `overflow-x` auto/scroll); clause (b) keeps plain clipping out by requiring an animated descendant. **And the other worked example falsified the first draft:** the marquee read M5s 0 because the audit runs under `reducedMotion: "reduce"` and the app's own `@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) { animation: none }` suppressed the property the predicate keyed on — animation is now read from the **stylesheet**, and `overflow-x: clip` is accepted alongside `hidden`. See the oddity and the SKILL clause. **Two M4 findings that were flagged "filed for the sweep" during P5/P6a and never got entries of their own are discharged by the same commit and recorded here so the trail is not lost:** the **height-keyed blindness** (HO 618 — `/stale`'s 52px `.row-hslot` was that route's whole M1 while M4 read nothing, because a horizontally-reserved slot is invisible to a `≥40px tall` emitter by construction) became **M4w**; the **short-text-not-empty** finding (HO 617 — `/member-detail`'s 2,312px was 27 populated elements) became the **M4-true / M4f** split. The third false-positive kind, **populated non-repeated chrome**, is a live caveat and not another instrument rev — see the layout-audit entry above. *Original shape, retained for the record —* *Owner:* Code, low. M5x as shipped captures **`text-overflow: ellipsis` and line clamps only**, so a container that scrolls by design lands in bucket (b) and reads as real narrow-width breakage. **Two worked examples, both measured:** the **markets marquee** on `/`, which contributes **~85 of `/`'s 86–88** at each narrow width because it is counted at six nesting levels (`markets-tape--scroll` 7519/976 · `-track` 7542/7538 · `-half` 3773/3769 · `-set` 1888/1885 · `-item` 179/175 · the label 131/59) — its overflow is the mechanism, not a defect (see the HO 606 oddity on marquee trailing gaps) — and **`pre.bdp-rawpre`** on `/bill/[id]` at 720 (710/686), the raw-JSON block, which is bucket (b)'s single entry on that route. **Deliberately not taken at HO 615:** §1 specified ellipsis and clamp, widening a measurement definition mid-close is how an instrument gets tuned rather than corrected, and the two examples are enumerable by hand today. *Shape when taken:* classify an element whose own computed `overflow-x` is `auto`/`scroll`/`hidden` **and** which carries an animated or explicitly-scrollable child — and **count the outermost of a nesting chain once**, since six levels of one marquee is the whole distortion. *Do not take it as part of a remediation HO* — it changes what the numbers mean, so it belongs in an instrument commit with its own falsification leg, and the fixture is where its positive and negative go.

Filed rather than taken because HO 645 was scoped to the tier and the handoff's §3 instruction was explicit (render the percentage alone if the denominator is absent from `stats` — it is). *Close:* the two counts ride `AbsentMember` and the back prints `<pct>% · <missed>/<total>`; or a measurement shows the denominator is not worth the two fields.~~ — **SHIPPED HO 656** (`9f3246d`, prod-verified). Closed exactly as the entry specified: `missedVotes`/`totalVotes` ride `AbsentMember` off the population pass's existing `p.total`/`p.not_voting`, and the back prints **`14.9% · 133/890`**. **Zero new SQL was the premise AND the gate** — added lines matching `db.execute|sql:` = **0**; had it needed a statement the section halted instead. Verified against a direct `member_participation` read (total=890 / not_voting=133 → M000355 McConnell), and the overflow stop-condition was **measured**: 83px on one line live, **93px at the mock's `29.3% · 370/1,262` scale**, inside the 330px back — so the mock-scale case the entry worried about fits too. **The row stays MISSED/TOTAL, not the mock's cast/total** (the numerator must be the quantity the percentage is a rate of). - ~~**Two more display orphans, extending the HO 627 chore (HO 649 probe, M1 inverse).** *Owner:* Code. The probe's inverse reading — **covered by SKILL but not reachable** — returns 4, of which two are correctly documented as removed (`TerminalPrompt`, `IssueBars`, both described in SKILL as superseded) and **two are presented as LIVE while rendering nowhere**: **`DashboardTopicTreemap`** (already in the HO 627 chore above) and **`StateFlag`** — which is **NEW: zero importers anywhere, and absent from backlog, oddities and roadmap entirely**, yet listed in SKILL's client-islands set as a live row affordance. **`IssueBars`** is also a dead FILE (mentioned only in two comments) though its SKILL prose is correct, so it joins the chore as a file to delete, not a passage to fix. Hand-verified by an independent method — direct `grep` for importers, not the probe's import graph — because the probe's own graph is the thing under test. **THIS READING WAS NOT IN THE PROBE'S SPEC — it exists because a column was added, and that is worth recording so the next probe's spec inherits it.** HO 649 §6 asked for the zero-hit split (no passage, does it render); the inverse — **covered by SKILL but not reachable** — was added on noticing that `DashboardTopicTreemap` passed its control as unreachable while never appearing in any output, because the zero-hit split only ever looks at components SKILL is SILENT about. A surface SKILL describes as live and no longer renders is invisible to the specified measurement by construction, and it is the same drift class as a passage describing a superseded version. **It is also the highest-value thing the probe produced**, and the one a later reader is most likely to cite, so: a coverage instrument needs BOTH directions — undocumented-and-live, and documented-and-dead. *Close:* both files removed with the HO 627 chore, under the same per-selector positive-control discipline.~~ — **SHIPPED HO 650** (`ded820a`), local-verified. **Closes completely, and wider than filed: all FOUR go, not two.** `DashboardTopicTreemap` (236 lines) · `IssueBars` (100) · `StateFlag` (22) · `TerminalPrompt` (41) — the two named here plus the two this entry had recorded as *correctly documented as removed*, which were still dead FILES. `TopicDatum` rehomed into `TopicDistributionList` first (two importers, `app/page.tsx:11` + the list's own line 5): the type edge is why the treemap outlived a chore already logged against it, since `import type` is erased at compile time and nothing complained. **The covered-but-not-reachable set goes 4 → 0** and the probe re-run confirms it, components **150 → 146**. Render diff on `/` is DOM-identical (clock-normalized — `/` cannot be byte-identical across requests, `nowMs` is per-request at six sites by HO 490 design), harness fired first so an empty diff means something, `tsc` + `next build` clean. **It also expired the probe's own C3a control** — that control anchored on the treemap being unreachable, and a deleted file is trivially unreachable, so it would have passed forever; re-anchored in the same commit to an inline fixture over `valueImports`, and proven able to fail. *See:* SKILL four sites (`123e4c3`).
- ~~**`fit-finish.spec.ts:143` pins a class the v2 feed row stopped rendering (found in passing, HO 654).** *Owner:* Code. The test asserts `a.bill-id-chip` inside `.v2f-group` and it **fails today**; the class is absent from the render entirely (0 occurrences against 30 `.v2f-group`), because `V2FeedList` renders `className="v2f-id"` — its own header records the swap, *“bordered `BillIdChip` → plain amber id text”*. **PRE-EXISTING and NOT caused by HO 654**, established by control rather than asserted: the pre-edit and post-edit renders are identical on both counts. **The failure mode is the one that matters here** — a committed test that cannot pass is indistinguishable, in a red run, from a test catching a real regression, and it trains the next reader to skim past a red `fit-finish`. *Close:* the assertion targets what the row actually renders, or the test is retired with its reason. **Do not “fix” it by re-adding the chip** — the row markup is the deliberate HO 609/613 compaction and the fixture is what drifted.~~ — **CLOSED HO 655** (`c201985`), and BOTH halves of the HO 654 entry were wrong. **THE FRAMING FIRST: there was no red run to skim past.** The entry said a committed test that cannot pass *“trains the next reader to skim past a red `fit-finish`”* — but **nothing in CI executes this spec**: `e2e-prod.yml` runs `e2e/smoke.spec.ts` by **explicit path** (no `e2e/` glob, deliberately) and `ci.yml` invokes no Playwright at all, both read off the workflow files at HO 655 §0. **That claim came from the reviewer's memory of CI coverage rather than from the workflow file**, which is the reasoning-over-reports class arriving in the entry that names it. **The mechanism RELOCATED rather than evaporating:** `npm run test:e2e` is `playwright test` with **no path**, so the default glob picks up BOTH specs — anyone running the documented manual command got a failure in `fit-finish` unrelated to whatever they were checking. The audience is a person at a terminal, not a workflow. **THE STATED CLOSE WAS ALSO WRONG, and that is what needed a handoff rather than a selector swap.** *“The assertion targets what the row actually renders”* produces a **still-failing test**: `.v2f-id` exists and is a real `<a href="/bill/…">`, so repointing satisfies `:143` and **fails four lines later at the navigation assertion**, where it reads as an app REGRESSION rather than as fixture rot and the next person spends the budget on the app. The behaviour was deliberately reversed by HO 609/613/627. **Measured on the running app** (not taken from the handoff or the component comment): **plain click → url unchanged, `.v2f-group.open` 1, tabs 1** · **ctrl → open 0, tabs 1→2, popup `/bill/119-s-3266`** · **middle → open 0, tabs 2→3, same popup**. The test now pins all three legs (real link · plain expands · modified opens a tab and does NOT also expand — the mirrored defect `rowLinkClick`'s `stopPropagation` guards, which had **no coverage at all** before). **Both new legs proven able to fire on scratch perturbations of HEAD, reverted:** strip `onClick` → **RED `:169`**; strip `stopPropagation` → **RED `:194`**. One perturbation would have proven only leg 2 and left the newly-added leg unfalsified. **`BillIdChip` is NOT deleted** — live at `components/BillIdChip.tsx:33` + `app/globals.css:4340`; HO 654's “0 occurrences” was scoped to `/` and correct there. **ROT RULING: (a) ACCEPT MANUAL ROT, plus this note.** **(b) a static selector check was declined on a MEASURED null, not a predicted one** — the HO 655 census (70 selectors, 41 distinct class tokens) returns **0 with no producer on the very suite that was carrying the defect**, so a static gate would have shipped **green over a broken test**. That is stronger than the handoff's prediction that it *“would not have caught this one”*, and it is the part that stops (b) being re-proposed: the class here is **selector exists, behaviour moved**, and no static check reaches it. **(c) a cadence tied to an existing event** is a convention nobody enforces, which is how it rotted. **AND THE OPTION NOBODY COSTED, recorded so it is DECLINED rather than omitted: running `fit-finish` unattended against LOCALHOST.** Running the test is the **only** thing that reaches the real class. The prod blocker is BotID, but that is a **prod-domain** reason and does not apply here; the localhost blocker is **different and unpriced** — the spec needs feed rows, so it needs `TURSO_*` credentials, and **`ci.yml` is secretless by deliberate decision at HO 488**. **NOT RECOMMENDED and unpriced:** taking it means reopening a property that was established on purpose, and nobody has costed the fixture/seeding work that would avoid doing so. Do not treat it as a bright idea; treat it as a trade someone must argue for.

No. N` — are real signal (amendments that died by tabling or never cleared cloture) but can't ride the "voted" cut's amendment-framed `VoteLine`: their yea/nay is on the motion, inverted or orthogonal to the amendment's fate, and `deriveDisposition` on their `votes.result` is misleading-on-tally (oddities). Needs a **motion-aware model** — invert tallies for a tabling, distinguish cloture from adoption, suppress when an anchored decisive vote already exists (`119-samdt-2360` etc.). Its own arc. *Gate:* appetite, not a fast-follow. *No fixed HO number* (HO 418). *Owner:* Corey.

- ~~**A deploy-liveness gate that stops at the FIRST matching read can green on a promotion transient.** *Owner:* Code. Observed landing `226bd99` (2026-08-12): a poll loop broke on a matching read of `/api/version`, and **the very next request served `daf06b5`** — two instances briefly on different builds mid-promotion. **Not a parse artifact:** the identical parse then read **12/12 clean** once promotion settled, which is what distinguishes a real transient from a broken harness. This is the convergence rule already in oddities (*A shipped VALUE change behind `unstable_cache` doesn't land on the first prod read — verify by convergence, not hit-count*, HO 555) **applied one layer down: to the SHA gate rather than to a cached value** — see that entry for the mechanism rather than a restatement here. **The placement is why it matters.** `/api/version` is the gate every post-deploy measurement runs behind, so a gate that false-greens **hands its doubt to everything taken after it** — a byte reading, a render diff, a latency sample all silently inherit “we were on the new build”. **`scripts/verify-deploy.ts` has exactly this shape today:** it polls until the first match and stops (that is its documented behaviour, not a regression). **Fix:** require a run of **N consecutive** matching reads before declaring live, not one. *Gate:* the next time a deploy check gates a measurement that matters — not worth a session before then. *Close:* `verify:deploy` requires N consecutive matches, or the transient is shown not to recur.~~ — **SHIPPED HO 653** (`0144f66`), and it closes on TWO observations rather than one, which is what makes it a shape instead of an anomaly. **(1) `226bd99` — BANKED, retrospective:** a matching read, then the very next request served `daf06b5`; caught by hand while landing, and filed on the strength of a single sighting. **(2) `4051206` — FIRED, AND CHANGED THE ANSWER:** read 9 matched, **read 10 served `f47b807`**, the run reset, and convergence came at read 15. Under the first-match shape the check exits green at read 9 — one request before prod served the predecessor again. That second instance is the citable evidence: the first showed the transient exists, the second showed the gate would have passed through it. `scripts/verify-deploy.ts` now requires **`REQUIRED_CONSECUTIVE = 5`**, and a break **prints the breaking SHA and resets the run** rather than continuing the count — a silent reset would hide the very event the check exists to catch. **Both branches were exercised before this was called done** (an untriggered guard is unproven): the confirm branch ran live against prod to 5 consecutive and exit 0; the break branch was falsified with a scratch stub sequencing match / match / other / matches, printing `BREAK — served f47b807deadbeef, run reset after 2/5` before restarting and confirming at 5/5, with the scratch reverted clean. **The general form, worth carrying:** the convergence rule (hit until it converges AND HOLDS) applies to a SHA gate exactly as it does to a cached value — and it matters more there, because `/api/version` is what every post-deploy measurement runs behind, so a gate that false-greens hands its doubt to every figure taken after it. Cost is ~40s of extra wall clock at a 10s interval. *Residual:* none — the by-hand convention and the unattended script now agree.

## WATCH

- ~~**The dashboard hearings card overlays its neighbour, and at 2560 the neighbour is the schedule's own second column (measured HO 730).** opened 2026-09-16; *Owner:* Corey, **gated** — an observation with a fork attached, not queued work. `HearingDetailCard` places itself right of its anchor and only flips when that overflows (`components/HearingDetailCard.tsx:71-73`). On `/` the right placement fits at every width read, so the HO 730 `/hearings` defect (flip → clamp → the card lands on its own anchor) **cannot occur here**: measured at `8712236`, local production build, @1440 row `L25 T574 R779 B599` / card `L781 T482 R1121 B892`, own-row overlap **0**, other schedule rows **0**; @2560 row `L25 T573 R710 B602` / card `L712 T573 R1052 B1090`, own-row overlap **0**, **12 right-column rows covered (120,360px²)** — re-read identical on HO 730's `feat` build (`scripts/diagnostic/card-overlap-730.ts`). At 1440 the same placement covers the MOVERS feed instead, which is ordinary popover behaviour and is not counted. **A below-the-anchor rule was specified and declined at the HO 730 STEP 0 ruling**: the card is 517px tall against a row at `T573`, so below-placement covers the rows under it in its own column — the same overlap with different rows — and a "covers any other row in this list" trigger would fire at 2560 and not at 1440 on an unchanged tree, keyed to what content sits beside the card rather than to a geometric failure. **The real question is whether a height-pinned glance panel should carry a floating card at all**, now that `/hearings` expands in flow and this is the component's only consumer. *Promote:* on Corey's word that the dashboard schedule converts too — which retires `HearingDetailCard`, its `.hcal-card*` CSS and `liveStatus`'s export site with it — or on a third surface adopting the card, at which point the placement is no longer one page's problem. *Reads if never done:* these rects, and one component with one consumer.~~ — **PROMOTED AND CLOSED HO 734: Corey's word arrived, the schedule converted, and the card is deleted.** The *Promote* branch above fired exactly as written — `HearingDetailCard`, its **nineteen** `.hcal-card*` rules and `liveStatus`'s export site all retired together. **The after is STRUCTURAL, not another rect table, and that is deliberate:** `.hcal-card` is absent from the tree (`grep -c hcal-card app/globals.css` → **0**), the panel is in flow, and `card-overlap-730.ts` reads `.hcal-card=0 .hearing-panel=1` with rows-under **0** and `over=0` at **1440, 2560, 430 and 390** — a floating element that no longer exists cannot cover a column, at any row count. **The geometry this entry recorded is HO 730's, and the proof is horizontal identity:** re-read at HO 734 STEP 0 on a local production build, the row and card sat at `L25 R779 / L781 R1121` @1440 and `L25 R710 / L712 R1052` @2560 — **the `:450` rects to the pixel**. The verticals differ (`T603/B878` against `T482/B892` @1440) and the 2560 overlap read **0 rows, 0px²** against twelve rows and 120,360px², **because the shown day carried ONE row**: `colB` is empty, so `.hsch-wrap` rendered a single child into its `1fr 1fr` grid and the card landed in the empty right track with nothing to cover. **The failure reading this entry's instrument was given — *"rects that differ mean the schedule moved"* — would have been the wrong conclusion here**, because it conflated geometry with content; the horizontal pair is the discriminator that separates them, and it is recorded here as such. **The *"height-pinned glance panel"* phrase in this entry was FALSE at close and is corrected:** HO 610 reversed HO 282's pinning (`app/globals.css:1088-1101`, *"Each panel sizes to its own content now"*) and HO 658 removed the `position: relative` the popover was held for, so C7 already governed that box and an in-flow panel that grows it is the behaviour a HEARINGS↔RACES click already had. **The two-column ruling, made here rather than left implicit:** the open panel grows its OWN column and the other column does not move — the panel's box measured **685px WIDE** at 2560 against its row's own **685px width**, so it stays inside its column rather than spanning the wrap and the two columns differ in HEIGHT, not width — because the panel belongs to its row and lifting it out to span both would restructure a grid HO 682 measured one level at a time. The **desync observation** that follows from it is owed and filed as its own WATCH line below. *Reads if never done:* these rects, and one component with one consumer.

What this line predicted — that the crawl exercises the pointer *until event 119474 leaves `hlig00`'s ten-meeting window* — was true of the 1280 smoke crawl and **never true of the narrow gate**: at ≤720px the pointer draws only stacked under a WATCH link, and measured at HO 740 all ten of `hlig00`'s recent meetings have `video_url IS NULL`, so at 430 and 390 it has been in the DOM and drawn nowhere since the day it shipped. **The limit, named rather than left to be over-read:** the fixture proves the pointer RENDERS at both widths in that form; its **correctness** (href, count) still rides the smoke crawl on the days content exists, and `/hearings` — the second surface — is uncovered and is filed on the residue line below.
- ~~**New (HO 669) · the live ODDS strip renders BOTH Fed-cut pairs as "FED CUT SEP" — the `showMonth` disambiguator has stopped disambiguating.** *Owner:* Code. **Observed, not inferred:** the `/` render captured for HO 669's roster gate (local production build, both pre- and post-chore, identical) reads its four ODDS pair labels as **`SHUTDOWN · FED CUT SEP · FED CUT SEP · RECESSION`**. HO 302 deliberately gave `FEDCUT` (July) and `FEDCUT-SEP` (September) the **same** `label: "FED CUT"` and relied on `showMonth` to tell them apart by appending the resolution month read from **each primary's own `marketDate`** — see `DashboardV2Header.tsx`'s `ODDS_PAIRS` and the SKILL `:1994` note. With the July decision now past, `FEDCUT`'s soonest-open Kalshi event has rolled forward, so its `marketDate` resolves to September too and both slots print the same string. **Unchanged by HO 669 and not caused by it** — the strip's two mounts were untouched and the pre/post renders are byte-identical — it was simply visible in the capture, which is the only reason it is on the record. **This is a WATCH rather than a fix because the mechanism may be self-correcting and the right fix depends on which:** if `FEDCUT`'s discovery has genuinely advanced to the September event, the two symbols are now **duplicates** and the roster wants one of them dropped, not a better label; if it is pinned elsewhere and only the displayed month is wrong, `showMonth` wants the fix. **Do not "fix" the label before establishing which** — appending a distinct month to two rows reading the same underlying market would make the tape confidently wrong instead of visibly redundant. **This is a WATCH and not a FIT & FINISH item because the fork is resolvable by WAITING, not by building** — `FEDCUT` has no pinned `kalshiEvent`, so its soonest-open discovery **will move on its own** when the next decision event opens, and whether the collision clears by itself is exactly what discriminates the two branches. *Close:* **on the next Kalshi `KXFEDDECISION` event opening, or on the September FOMC resolving — whichever lands first** — re-read the two symbols' `market_ticks.marketDate` and the discovered event ticker for each, then take the branch the reading names. **(a) The collision CLEARS** — `FEDCUT` has rolled to a later event than `FEDCUT-SEP`, the labels read distinct months again, and the entry closes as self-resolved with the reading recorded; this is the expected outcome and it means `showMonth` was working correctly the whole time, on a roster that had gone momentarily degenerate. **(b) The collision PERSISTS** — both symbols resolve to the same event, which makes them **duplicates**, not a labelling bug: the fix is to drop one from `ODDS_TAPE`/`ODDS_PAIRS` (and decide whether `FEDCUT-SEP`'s `kalshiEvent` pin still earns its place), NOT to append a distinguishing month to two rows reading one market. **Do not relabel before this read resolves** — that would make the tape confidently wrong instead of visibly redundant, which is strictly worse.~~ — **CLOSED HO 681, branch (b), on the reading the entry itself demanded.** Re-read 2026-09-01 against live upstream, not the DB alone: **`FEDCUT` and `FEDCUT-SEP` both resolve `KXFEDDECISION-26SEP`; `POLY-FEDCUT` and `POLY-FEDCUT-SEP` both resolve `fed-decision-in-september-762`**, with K 2.0% / P 0.7% on both halves — **the collision PERSISTED**, so the two symbols are **duplicates and `FEDCUT-SEP`'s `kalshiEvent` pin does not earn its place**. The entry's instruction was followed exactly: the pinned pair was dropped from `ODDS_TAPE`/`ODDS_PAIRS` (and from `/welcome`'s own strip, which the entry did not know about — it labels from `MARKET_SYMBOLS[].label` and so read `FED CUT ODDS` / `FED CUT ODDS (SEP)`, **visually distinct while carrying identical numbers**, which is the harder duplication to notice), and **no distinguishing month was appended to two rows reading one market**. **`showMonth` is exonerated** — it faithfully reported that both pairs had the same resolution month; it stays ON and now names which meeting the roller sits on, rolling to FED CUT OCT after 2026-09-16 by itself. Two corrections the entry could not have made: the two symbols' stored `marketDate` **differed** (`2026-09-16` vs `2026-09-01`) because the pinned path defaults the ticker day to 01, so a `marketDate` comparison alone would have read them as distinct; and the quantity that actually settles it is **identity**, which nothing in the pipeline reported until HO 681 added `resolvedId` and the per-source collision flag. Also on the record: **the entry's own premise that the fork was resolvable by WAITING was correct** — the roller moved on its own, exactly as written.

Source-side corroboration, since the console line cannot be read from here: **`grep '[sync] lead'` = 0** and **`lead:` in `app/api/sync/route.ts` = 0**; every surviving `weekly_lead`/`getDashboardLead` mention in the tree is an HO 667 RECORD comment (`queries.ts:1071–1072`, `migrate.ts:80`, `primaries-sync.ts:1864`, `sync-timeout-verify-480.ts`). **Two instrument failures were caught and paid for before any zero was trusted, and both would have closed this WATCH on nothing:** (1) `timings` is **not** at the payload root — the envelope is `{ok, elapsedMs, payload:{timings,…}}`, so reading `p.timings` returned "no timings" for **every** row, pre- and post-deploy alike, which reads exactly like "the lead is gone"; (2) string-comparing an ISO instant to a space-separated boundary is not a date comparison (`"…T18:01Z" > "… 21:44"` because `T` > space), which misclassified all four rider-baseline pre-deploy ticks as post-deploy. The fix is the **control** the entry now carries: a pre-deploy tick must print a **numeric** lead on the same invocation before any post-deploy zero counts — 15 of 16 did (the 16th, #9165, is a `timeout` row with no timings object at all). Probe kept untracked at `scripts/diagnostic/sync-lead-watch-669.ts` with both traps written into its header.

Against the 15,481 B post-HO-631 reading that is **0.14x**, so the WATCH this entry re-priced (**bytes-per-member, not member count**) is satisfied for now and stays worth re-reading when either moves. **Rendered output is unchanged, and that is the load-bearing check rather than the byte figure**: the served `.abw-bk-*` subtrees are byte-identical for both members (md5 `7308325226933bfc`), all four `+N` closers unmoved at +11 / +21 / +4 / +9 — a broken `cmteMore` is exactly what a plausible-looking render would hide, which is why committees are projected but **NOT sliced** (that closer counts off the full array; `recentBills` is sliceable only because HO 631 moved its closer onto `stats.total`). **`ABSENCE_BACK_BILLS_CAP` = 3** is the shared cap now, and `lib/member-card-caps.ts` no longer describes this band at all — its header carried two clauses this change falsified. **NOT closed here, and named rather than dropped:** the **SQL-side count for `getSponsorTopTopics`** is still unclaimed (it has survived two entries in this lineage as a “then consider”; `topics` no longer reaching this payload removes the symptom on THIS surface, not the JS-side count `/members` still runs). **No prod byte figure is reported here, and the reason is NOT the one §4 gives** — this is a correction of that premise, not an inheritance of it. §4 says the key is the `keyParts` literal, so the fat blob would keep serving and shrink only on regeneration. So **concurrency is evaluated BEFORE the job `if`**, and a run that does no work at all still cancels the run doing the work. The cancellation window is therefore **any `deployment_status` event landing during the crawl's ~3 minutes**, not the "deploy burst" this entry assumed, and it is reachable by an ordinary push to any branch rather than only by a production deploy. Re-dispatched clean as `33673524519`. **This now blocks something concrete:** the HO 683 overflow alarm is armed on `schedule`, so a cancelled scheduled run files nothing and the alarm silently misses a day. *Promote from WATCH — it bit.* Cheapest fix to weigh first: make the group per-event (`e2e-prod-${{ github.event_name }}`) or drop `cancel-in-progress` for non-`deployment_status` events.~~ — **CLOSED HO 685 (`88d776f`), and the sharper instance is the one to lead with: the alarm's blind window was not hypothetical, it swallowed a day.** **A three-run cancellation chain, 2026-09-02 18:25–18:31, three runs and ZERO completed crawls in six minutes:** `33667151345` (a working `deployment_status` crawl, job 18:25:58) killed at 18:28:14 by `33667308181`, **the ARMED `0 15 * * *` daily** (GitHub-delivered 18:27, best-effort per HO 572); which was itself killed at 18:31:04, **2m46s in**, by `33667584259` — a Preview `deployment_status` run whose job then **skipped** at 18:31:05. So the overflow alarm filed nothing for 2026-09-02 and that day is **indistinguishable from a quiet prod** — the blind window realized on its actual carrier, and precisely what Corey reported as a live fire. **The prior instance, restated with its figure corrected:** dispatch `33673317612` was **cancelled 89s into its ~3-minute crawl** by `33673395814`, a run created **47s after it** — the filed "47s in" measures the gap between the two runs' *creation* timestamps, not depth into the crawl. Re-dispatched clean as `33673524519`. **The fix is the GROUP KEY, not the cancel flag**: `e2e-prod-${{ github.event_name }}-${{ …deployment_status.environment || 'na' }}-${{ …deployment_status.state || 'na' }}` — the job `if`'s own three discriminators, so the group partitions along the same line the skip decision does and every skipped class collides only with itself. **Disposition of all four cancellations in the 72h sweep under the new key:** the 09-01 `33561461443` pair is **preserved** (both Production+success — identical key inputs, same group, the documented deploy-burst collapse, which was the original purpose and is byte-unchanged within its group); chain links 1, 2 and 3 above are all **prevented**. **`state` is kept for a totality property, not a live distinction** — measured at STEP 0 it is a constant `success` across the 62-status corpus (Preview 36 / Production 26), and what it buys is that a group is working iff `event != deployment_status OR (environment == Production AND state == success)`, exactly the key's three discriminators, so a working and a skipped run cannot share a group **for any value Vercel might emit**, including states never observed. **Per-event-only was weighed and rejected:** it closes the alarm's window but leaves a skipped Preview run able to cancel a working Production crawl inside a shared deploy group — same mechanism, narrower victim. **Proven pre-FF, and the proof is real rather than trivial because one handoff premise was false:** `deployment_status` runs check out the **deployment's own ref**, not `main` (`33673395814` ran at `5c9503a` on `683-review`), so the branch already exercised the end-state key on both sides — dispatch `33709162259` logged its group as **`e2e-prod-workflow_dispatch-na-na`**, ran armed, and completed **success, uncancelled**, while `33709247204` (a Preview event pushed mid-crawl at 02:52:12, inside its 02:50:53–02:54:35 job window) **skipped**. Pre-fix that exact shape is the incident. **Transition residual, bounded and harmless:** deploys of pre-fix commits run the old yaml under bare `e2e-prod`, and group matching is exact-string, so they can only cancel each other and can never reach a new-key group.

The original text follows, kept because the reasoning is what earned the instruments. ORIGINAL: *Owner:* Code. Fifteen sites are gated by `html[data-odds="off"]` via `.odds-only` / `.odds-off-only`; a sixteenth added later simply keeps rendering when a reader has turned the markets off. **Nothing catches it automatically, and the reason is structural: the unattended crawl never sets the preference, so CI only ever measures the ON path** — where an ungated site is, by definition, correct. The typechecker cannot see a missing class, and the ON-path baseline (`scripts/diagnostic/artifacts-692/on-*.json`) is designed to prove the ON render did NOT change, so it stays green too. **Instrument:** re-run the row-2 enumeration at each HO touching races, the tapes, or the bill panel — `grep -rni "kalshi\|polymarket\|SourceTag\|impliedPct\|favoriteLabel" components app --include=*.tsx` — and classify every hit either into the gated set or into "not gated" with a reason; **zero means no ungated market site**, and at HO 692 the four hits outside the table were a news `sourceTag()` false positive, a comment, and two `KalshiLine` consumers already covered by the shared root class. **Second instrument, for the one site a grep cannot judge:** `scripts/diagnostic/odds-leader-fallback-692.ts`, which renders `RaceCard` through `react-dom/server` and asserts the `leader` line's dual render — that the market reading sits inside `odds-only` and the fallback is byte-identical to a real `nolead` card's own challenger line. **That harness is the ONLY check on that branch**, because there is no `leader` seat on the live dashboard to capture (all six featured cards are general/nominee/nolead; `.rc-dagger` counts zero), and it will stay that way while primaries keep resolving — so its absence from a future run is not evidence the branch is fine. **What a zero does NOT mean:** it does not mean a gated site still WORKS — a site can carry the class and be defeated by a later CSS rule or a grid re-flow — which is what the geometry baseline and the OFF capture are for. *Close criterion:* not a loop that closes on a number; it expires if the preference is ever removed, or if the crawl is taught to run a second pass with the preference set (which would move this from a review rule to a gate, and is the fix worth making if a site is ever found missing).

- ~~**New (HO 694) · the `/lobbying` fbar controls overlap at 430 — pre-existing, surfaced by this work and deliberately not fixed.**~~ — **CLOSED at HO 694 (`303b6f8`), same HO, because the narrow gate's FIRST CI RUN proved it was not the cosmetic-only item this line called it.** Filed as a visual overlap that `narrow.spec.ts` correctly read green over; CI then failed `/lobbying` at 430 by 7px on `input.lob-fbar-search-input`, and a 380-760 sweep against prod showed the real defect is `.lob-fbar-controls` overflowing the bar's content box at **every width from 380 to 460** (+54 / +43 / +34 / **+25** / +8), with the document itself scrolling at 380 and 400 — two widths below the gate's own. The overlap and the overflow are one cause: a non-wrapping flex row of three controls inside a bar that wraps around it. `flex-wrap: wrap` plus a full-line search below 700 closes both; sweep now reads 0 over at every width. **The line was not wrong to be filed — it was wrong about severity, and only a second host's font metrics showed why** (Windows read the route 430/430 on the same SHA CI failed it on). *(Original entry follows.)*  *Owner:* Code, low. With the pane stacked (HO 694) the filter bar is finally visible on a phone, and it is wrong: the BILL-LINKED toggle sits **on top of** the RECENT/VOLUME segmented control, which reads as `RECENT | VOLU` with the rest covered. **Pre-existing and unrelated to the stacking** — it is identical in the BEFORE capture, where the 84px content column made the whole bar unreadable anyway, so the stack revealed it rather than caused it (`docs/handoffs/694-artifacts/BEFORE-lobbying-430.png` vs `AFTER-lobbying-430.png`, both local-only). **It is NOT a doc-scroll defect and `e2e/narrow.spec.ts` correctly reads green over it**, which is the honest scope of that gate and worth saying here so a future reader does not take a green narrow run as a claim about legibility. *Close:* give `.mc-fbar` a narrow-width treatment (wrap the control groups rather than letting them share a line), or record a keep reason. Not fixed at HO 694 because it is a different class from the seven overflow reds that HO existed to close, and folding it in would have made the scope widening unbounded rather than merely stated.

Read in the RECORD form for both: **`bareTape` 0 live / 1 RECORD, `BARE_TAPE_EXCLUDED` 0 live / 1 RECORD**, the single RECORD being the rewritten `MarketsTape.tsx` header that names both as struck. **The comment was kept rather than gutted to satisfy the substring count** — a RECORD names the dead thing, which is what makes it a record and not a silence (`queries.ts:1071`'s "RECORD (HO 667): `getDashboardLead` lived here" is the house form), and gutting it is precisely the failure the "not gutted to satisfy a substring count" clause exists to prevent. The const itself is gone; the only survivor is prose about its removal. **No per-render read was removed and no pixel changed — the win is dead code and one retired data tag** (−71 / +48 across 2 files); say it plainly rather than dressing it up. **SKILL took THREE edits, not the one the handoff predicted** — `:1997` as specced (and it also carried the `bareTape`/`BARE_TAPE_EXCLUDED` tokens the recon said appeared nowhere in SKILL), plus `:1994` and `:2000`, each carrying a clause the strip falsifies; approved as an amendment to the table rather than forced. **`:2000`'s first cut then had to be cut back, and the reason belongs in this entry because it is the entry's own subject matter.** It read "so the scope now guards nothing: both surviving mounts pass `scroll`" — an inference, not a reading: the verified half is that both mounts pass `scroll`, while "guards nothing" additionally requires knowing the scope's predicate, which nothing in the HO had measured. Corey held it on exactly that ground — **writing an unverified SKILL claim while closing an entry about unverified SKILL claims** — and offered the fork: quote the rule, or cut to the verified half. **Measuring it did not merely fail to support the claim, it FALSIFIED it.** `app/globals.css:6678`, `:6691` and `:6970–6971` scope real overrides (item padding `0 11px`; the `::after` pipe; the 28px → 72px edge fade) whose **unscoped** values are the tight static defaults — and `globals.css:6674–6675` already records why the scoping stays: "`scroll` is still an opt-in prop on `MarketsTape`." **HO 669 did not change that** — `scroll` still defaults `false`, so the unscoped path stays reachable by the component's API and merely has no current caller. The scope guards something. `:2000` was cut to the verified half and now records the strip and nothing more. **No `:2087` changelog append** — `MarketsTape` is a server component and `MarketsTapeClient` is untouched, so client-island membership does not change (HO 665's criterion, and HO 666 is the inverse case).

- ~~**P2 · `/committee/[systemCode]` (+ `/trends`) · uncaught React hydration mismatch (#418) on every committee page.**~~ **RESOLVED HO 473** (`fd74357`) — collapsed the four multi-child SVG `<title>` sites to a single child (`CommitteeActivityChart` + `BillsTimeSeries` were the live/latent throwers; `PrimaryDistrictModal` + `RaceDistrictModal` fixed for class-consistency, byte-identical text). `/committee/[systemCode]` + `/trends` now console-clean, no #418; `grep "<title>"` shows only single-child titles. *Original:* loaded `/committee/hlig00` (+ `ssju00`, `hsju00`) in a real browser; each throws an uncaught `#418` ("Hydration failed because the server rendered HTML didn't match the client. As a result this tree will be regenerated on the client"). Data-independent (all 3 committees). *Root cause (dev-trace-pinned):* `components/CommitteeActivityChart.tsx:145-147` — the bar `<title>` has **multiple adjacent JSX children** `{formatMonthLabel(month)} · {bucket} · {value}` inside an SVG `<title>`; React's SSR text-segment markers don't survive the browser's SVG-`<title>` parse → hydration fails → React client-regenerates the chart subtree (flash + wasted client work; no wrong data shown, hence P2 not P1). The **same idiom is in `components/BillsTimeSeries.tsx:124`** (`{formatMonthLabel(month)} · {topicLabel(topic)} · {value}`), so `/trends` carries the same latent bug (not exercised locally — force-dynamic corpus page). `PrimaryTimeline`'s `<title>` uses a single template-literal child → unaffected — which is the fix shape: collapse each to one string child, `{`${formatMonthLabel(month)} · ${bucket} · ${value}`}`. *Reproduced on the local prod build; near-certainly live on Vercel prod (same build output).*
- ~~**P2 · `/lobbying` · pagination silently drops the selected `?issue=` drill.**~~ **RESOLVED HO 473** (`cc416fa`) — `app/lobbying/page.tsx` now builds a `carry` from the raw `params.issue` (only when present, so it never injects `?issue=` on the unfiltered feed) and hands it to `<Pagination>`, matching `/amendments` + `/nominations`. *Original:* loaded `/lobbying?issue=HCR`, clicked NEXT in the recent-filings pager → URL became `/lobbying?page=2` (the `issue=HCR` is gone). The Section-2 IssueDrill then re-selects the default top issue (`issues[0]`), so a reader paging the feed loses the per-code drill they were reading. *Root cause:* `app/lobbying/page.tsx` passes `carry={new URLSearchParams()}` (empty) to `<Pagination>`, unlike `/nominations`, `/amendments`, and `/trades` — all of which pass a populated `carry` that preserves their filters across pagination. So `/lobbying` is the lone aggregate surface that drops its filter param on NEXT. *Close:* build the `carry` from `issue` (mirror the other three surfaces).
- ~~**P3 · `/` feed row expand · Esc does not close an expanded row.**~~ **RESOLVED HO 473** (`156dfe5`) — added an Escape `keydown` effect to `components/useSingleOpenPanel.ts` (guarded on `expandedId === null`), so all four consumers (`BillRowList`, `TopStallsList`, `V2FeedList`, `CompetitiveRacesStrip`) inherit Esc-to-close; the electoral modal's independent handler is untouched. *Original:* expanded a `.v2f-row`, pressed Escape — the row stays open (`.v2f-group.open` count = 1). The single-open invariant and re-click-to-close both work correctly; **only Esc is a no-op.** *Root cause:* `components/V2FeedList.tsx` / `useSingleOpenPanel` wires Enter/Space to toggle but has no Escape-to-close handler. Note the app is internally inconsistent here — the electoral `RaceDistrictModal` DOES close on Esc (verified), the inline feed expand doesn't. Arguably an inline accordion doesn't require Esc (hence P3), but the HO 472 brief named "re-click / Esc closes" as a thing to verify, and Esc fails. *Close:* add an Escape keydown to close the open panel, or accept as by-design.

## DONE

- ~~**`BillExpandPanel` collapses in a narrow container (HO 609 finding → HO 610)**~~ — **SHIPPED** (`2ca2c96`), prod-verified at `a620e20`. Inside `/`'s 400px feed column the panel is 372px and its 1.35fr/1fr body resolved to **99px / 273px — 71px of prose, 1.4 words per line over 48 lines**. **The fr ratio was not the cause:** the metabox's ~273px min-content is a floor the 1fr track cannot go under, so the entire squeeze lands on the summary — worth knowing before tuning fr values on any two-column panel. Fixed with a **container query on the panel's own width** (stacks below ~440px), NOT a viewport query and NOT a raised column floor: `/bills` renders the same component at 1108–2228px and never crosses the threshold, so one component serves two containers with no route branch. After: **6.9 words/line over 10 lines** on `/`, `/bills` unchanged in shape. **The container query also made `.bxp` a containing block for absolute descendants** — the sponsor hover card lives inside it and was re-checked (still resolves against `.bxp-sponsor`, still on screen).
- ~~**SC phantom Democratic special primary (HO 600)**~~ — **SHIPPED** (`36b7731`; STEP 0 `43b7ef6`), prod-verified. Deleted `senate-SC-2026-special-D`, a live row asserting a contest that does not exist: SC's Aug 11 primary is single-party under **S.C. Code §7-11-55** because the deceased Republican nominee had general-election opposition (verified 2026-08-05, SC Election Commission notice + Ballotpedia corroboration). **Not dormant** — it fed `getPrimaryCalendar` -> `PrimaryTimeline` and `getDashboardPrimaries`, inflating the Aug 11 tick **48 -> 47** contests; the footprint was a **count inflation, not a rendered label**. Both halves in one commit (the seed loader has no delete pass, so either half alone is undone by the other); delete was one literal id, print-before-delete, dry-run default, zero-candidate gate enforced at runtime. Seed note rewritten with the statute, its sources and the verification date. See roadmap "Also (HO 600-601)". Docs: HO 602.
- ~~**SC Aug 11 roster ingestion — the votebox router (HO 601)**~~ — **SHIPPED** (`5bf1176` pre-flight · `efa858f` code · `0473dc6` docs · `07dd555` baseline relabel · `c4a3c06` freeze-status residual), prod-verified, **10 rows on `senate-SC-2026-special-R`, exact name match, 0 missing / 0 extra**. Replaced the page-type gate (`box-special === page-special`, which inverted against itself because `isSpecialElectionPage()` reads the `<title>` and Ballotpedia serves the FL/OH special article at the *regular* URL) with **`<h5>` classification routed against the seed registry**: a special box goes to `senate-{ST}-2026-special-{party}` **iff seeded**, else to the base id. **§1's own rule was rejected on the §2 gates** — routing on the `<h5>` alone produced 4 unseeded targets and would have frozen FL and OH silently. Also: specialness-aware dedup key (Mark Lynch runs in both SC contests), the C2 regular-page fallback kept for a **cursor-driven** reason (the senate slice may not reach SC before Aug 11), the special pass filtered to `isSpecial` so it writes only its invoked ids, and C3's result object replacing a `string[]` that **could not read FAIL**. Three falsification legs, run twice (idempotency 10 -> 10). See roadmap "Also (HO 600-601)". Docs: HO 602.

Margin -4.1s uses the 14.1s real reading, and is itself a lower bound on how negative it gets)** because page residency is under 60s; HO 595 **materialized to `member_participation`** and `member_votes` is now absent from the request path. *Verification, query-level and co-located:* **GREEN worst 6.46s (margin +3.54s) vs RED 14.4s (margin -4.4s) measured in the same requests; 0 query breaches >=10s, 0 TimeoutErrors** across 7 cold runs; the residual 2-6s proven **positional** (the first-query-in-a-cold-function tax — reordering moved it, and the same read runs 23-137ms when it follows). Act 7 of the 2026 Regular Session restored LA's jungle U.S.-House system (verified 2026-07-31): `HOUSE_PRIMARY_SUSPENDED` (the force-NULL lock) → a per-state **`HOUSE_PRIMARY_OVERRIDES`** map, LA → `{2026-11-03, 2026-12-12, jungle}`, an override winning over `calByState` (no COALESCE), suspension re-expressible as a NULL-dated override. **The one-line "unlock" the old item asked for was necessary and insufficient** — unlocking alone would have COALESCEd the clean May-16 senate date onto the six House rows, the HO 577 clobber through the front door, so the fix generalized the mechanism. - ~~**`LDA_API_KEY` absent from Vercel Production → anonymous 429/504 lobbying cron (resolved, recorded HO 581)**~~ — **RESOLVED** (env fix, not code): the key was added to Vercel Production and the 504s are gone. Recorded because the **mechanism is the instructive half** — `lib/lda-sync.ts:115` (`authHeaders`) attaches `Authorization: Token ${key}` **only when the key exists**, so a keyless cron ran **anonymously**, got **429'd**, slept **39s inside `fetchPage`** past the outer `:411` deadline check, and was **SIGKILLed at 300s** → the observed 504. **The env gap was the trigger; the deadline hole was the mechanism** — the key fixed the first, **HO 580 Change 2** fixed the second (the retry is now deadline-aware; see roadmap "Also (HO 579–580)"). No backlog line ever tracked this (it surfaced in the HO 579 ship report); tombstoned here for the record. Docs: HO 581.

Suite **1 failed → 0 failed** (41 pass / 1 skip); `inView` true at default + short viewport, `?issue=ZZZZ` fallback 200 / anchor-present / 0-console (prod-confirmed), page height unmoved. **STEP 0(a):** the `0 15 * * *` `schedule` trigger **fires** (2026-07-29 fail / 07-30 success, both ~85 min after the slot); latest green **closes the Jul-23 open loop and the whole Phase-2 Playwright-in-CI loop** (its one surviving thread). - ~~**Committee_members non-empty delete gate (HO 568)**~~ — **SHIPPED** (`38ea8b0`), prod-verified. The 564 rule ported to the committees standing-wipe, this time with the analogy **measured first** (566 M1 sized the same shape). Per-committee DELETE gated on the **insertable count** (bioguide-bearing entries, not raw array length), with a rostered/unrostered **refusal fork** — a code with existing rows whose incoming roster is empty/insertless reports in `rosterDeletesRefused`, an unrostered one skips silently (keeps the field signal-bearing at `[]`; the 10 zero-member committees are **source-absent**, never entering the loop, so the silent-skip branch is purely defensive today). One-`db.batch` shape preserved (HO 143). **Falsified both variants on `hsag00`** — 53 rows byte-identical under an empty array AND under insertless entries (proving it keys on insertable count, not length); control `hsju00` rewrote normally; natural run restored `[]`. Dissolution trade accepted (a genuinely emptied roster lingers at last membership until a manual one-off). **Two files** (flagged): the committees route hand-picks payload fields, unlike the primaries pass-through, so surfacing the field took one line in `app/api/cron/committees/route.ts` — guard #1 vs the §5.2 payload gate, resolved toward the gate. - ~~**member_votes self-heal (HO 567)**~~ — **SHIPPED** (diag `26879b5` · C1 `547d358` · C2 `f6aab1b`), prod-verified. Closes the zero-roster inflow race (tally publishes before the member positions; the WINDOWED watermark then freezes the empty roster). **STEP 0 gated the cause** — all four zero-roster votes returned populated rosters upstream with tallies matching stored exactly (transient publication, not memberless rolls). Chosen: a **bounded self-heal pass** over holding the vote row (tallies stay live, permanent loss → bounded lag, repair + fix one mechanism — the first run drained the 4, no backfill). Predicate **zero-roster only** (`tally>0 AND NOT EXISTS member_votes`, newest-first, `HEAL_CAP=10`/chamber) — self-limiting (a partial heal exits the set), so no deficit-predicate spin; reuses the per-vote write unchanged; permanent retry (553). **Both branches proven** (549/553): scratch-forced `269` → `pending`, row unchanged, `253`/`254` healed live; revert clean; re-run healed. Drain: all four to full tally, zero residual (the 566 probe re-ran as the regression instrument — M2(a) 0/0). **Participation distortion (per the §0.4 read):** a zero-roster vote **deflates** the 119th participation denominator — `getParticipationStrip` / `participationAggCte` / `getChamberParticipationContext` compute `not_voting / COUNT(*)` over the member's OWN `member_votes`, so a vote with no rows is absent from every member's count (not a per-member MISSED inflation); the career path (`member_career_votes`, Voteview) is unaffected. **Render payoff:** `senate-119-2-146` (Kim Amdt 5545 budget waiver) motion-line split — all-zero → HO 533-suppressed — now renders 44 D + 2 I yea / 53 R nay / 1 D nv. `memberVotesHealed` / `memberVotesHealPending` → payload (id 6056). No theme-% change. See roadmap "Also (HO 566–568)" + oddities + SKILL. Docs: HO 569.

- ~~**House roster-erasure fix + repair (HO 564)**~~ — **SHIPPED** (`154d779` C1 · `95fdd10` C2), prod-verified, main `95fdd10`. The rule: *a sync must never convert absence of evidence into deletion.* `syncHouseDistricts`' status fork is now **four-way** — `special`/`no_page`/`no_section` all skip the write block (a missing `Candidates_and_election_results` anchor after three retries is a parse failure, not evidence a contest exists), `no_candidates` stays on the row-creating path (that page parsed) — and the per-contest `DELETE`+insert runs **only when the incoming roster is non-empty**, on BOTH chambers. Senate widened on **structure not measurement** (its fork already skipped non-`ok`; STEP 0 S3 sized the empty-per-contest-roster edge at **28 rows** outside the HO 560 freeze), composing with that freeze as **two independent refusals** — settled-freeze refuses a *rewrite* (substitution), the non-empty gate refuses a *delete backed by nothing* (erasure). `rosterDeletesRefused` on both summaries + into `cron_runs.payload`. **Falsified before recorded as protection** (HO 549/553): MD-05 — forced `no_section` → roster byte-identical 27→27 + skip logged; forced empty-`ok` → `rosterDeletesRefused` naming both contests; scratch reverted, nothing committed. **Repair, measured:** House candidate rows **1,911→2,294** (+383) · contests-with-roster **594→718** (+124) · contests-carrying-a-share **389→410** (+21, result sets recovered not just rosters) · zero-candidate **143→73**; refusals NE 44 · S 34 · MW 1 · W 7. Residual 73 decomposed (40 legit-uncontested-with-rostered-sibling + 32 genuinely non-parsing across 16 districts — WATCH), not stuck. Post-re-enable confirm tick `cron_runs` id 6042 `rosterDeletesRefused: []` — wiring **live in the prod payload**, not guard-evidence (that's the MD-05 falsification). No theme-% change. See roadmap "Also (HO 563–564)" + oddities ×2. Docs: HO 565.

- ~~**House settled-row clobber guard (HO 560 follow-on)**~~ — **CLOSED at HO 564**, and the queued framing was **wrong**: it was banked as the HO 560 settled-freeze predicate *ported across to the House*, but HO 563 M3 showed the House failure is **erasure** (a non-parsing scrape deleting a real roster), not **substitution** (rewriting a settled row around a later contest's field) — a freeze keyed on "already carries a share" cannot protect a row whose shares are what gets deleted. So it shipped as a **different guard entirely** (the non-empty-roster delete gate), not the ported predicate. - ~~**Motion-aware procedural-amendment-outcome surface (HO 537 M8 → GO'd 550 → STEP-0'd 554 → SHIPPED 557)**~~ — **SHIPPED** (`568d58d` leaf · `7027d10` query · `b40b462` surface; diagnostics `7798997` / `d213cb7` / `70a23ae`), prod-verified at `bf820bd`. The 64 Senate roll calls that touch an amendment without being its up-or-down vote now render a motion line — motion, tally, party split, `/vote/[id]` drill, and a fate clause where the motion decides; **60 amendments gain a line** — 55 with a "→ amendment killed" clause, the rest tally-only; the 4 anchored are suppressed and **none of them are killed rows** (a motion-killed amendment never receives its own up-or-down vote, so suppression can't hide a fate — HO 558 M2). **The STEP 0 gate fired and changed the model.** HO 554's DISAGREE=0 tripwire had been measured against the pre-555 classifier; reconciling the probe's pinned `deriveDisposition` copy (the HO 556 WATCH, discharged) took it 0→2 on `119-samdt-3946`/`3947` — the pair HO 550's 54/56 polarity validation missed. Cause: `impliedFate` described the amendment's status **at the motion**, the dot describes its **outcome**; a failed tabling motion leaves an amendment pending, and it can still die later (both fell with their parents). Fixed by narrowing to **`motionFate → "killed" | "undecided" | "orthogonal"`** — **no positive fate**, since tabling and waiver are kill mechanisms that decide in one direction only (tabling agreed → killed, waiver failed → killed, opposites → undecided). Re-gate `dot=agreed AND fate=killed` measured **0**; `killed=55 · undecided=4 · orthogonal=5`. Rejected: comparing fate against the dot at render time (couples the motion model to `deriveDisposition`'s vocabulary — a future widening would silently change behavior). **No writes, no migration, no index, no sync/cron touch, Senate only, dot untouched, `getAmendments` filter semantics untouched** (hero byte-identical: 336/254/35 + 187 voted). **No theme-% change** — combined bar holds at 92. See roadmap "Also (HO 557)" + oddities ×2. Docs: HO 558.

HO 554's motion-model STEP 0 found the prod dot rendering muted `other` on 53/64 procedural-residual rows whose `latest_action_text` states an unambiguous fate; HO 555 refused to patch the examples and clustered the FULL `other` bucket (115/625 acted): the failed-family gains anchored "ruled out of order" + word-boundaried "fell", rescuing **80/115** (agreed 337→336 · failed 173→254 · otherActed 115→35). Withdrawn / rendered-moot / cloture-not-invoked deliberately stay `other`; "stricken" is not a kill token. Zero collision with the 4-string `votes.result` vocabulary, so one shared function serves both input families. **One accepted flip:** `119-samdt-3963` agreed→failed — the OLD read was the wrong one (a trailing "agreed to" from a subordinate "fell when …" clause; the failed-first ordering now decides it — oddities). Voted block untouched (0 flips); the `?disposition=` filter + voted EXISTS bypass `deriveDisposition` (M4 grep), so filter semantics never moved. M5 (the subordinate-clause instrument) reads 0 → WATCH. **No theme-% change** — combined `Lobbying / amendments / nominations` bar holds at 92. See roadmap "Also (HO 554–555)". Docs: HO 556.

probe `9383b9f`), prod cache-hit verified. `lib/amendment-votes-walk.ts` (HO 532) linked EVERY `recordedVote` off a HAMDT's `/actions` with no question filter (the mirror of the anchored Senate `SENATE_AMDT_Q`), so `119-hamdt-40`→`house-119-1-187` and `119-hamdt-175`→`house-119-2-122` rendered an "On Ordering the Previous Question" procedural tally through the amendment-framed `VoteLine` as the amendment's own outcome (green agreed dot from `deriveDisposition("Passed")`) — wrong on the number and the outcome. **HO 551:** the decisive-form gate `HOUSE_AMDT_QUESTION_LIKE = "On Agreeing to the Amendment%"` — derived from the **full 92-row enumeration** (89 "On Agreeing to the Amendment" + 1 ", as Modified" + 2 procedural, vocabulary CLOSED), NOT the two offenders — added to `lib/amendment-vote-key.ts` beside the Senate one (the asymmetry survived because the House had no counterpart there); applied via a JOIN to `votes` in the INSERT's LIKE; the 2 bad rows deleted one-time, scoped to the enumerated procedural set (dry-run confirmed exactly 2), both offenders keeping their real "On Agreeing to the Amendment" vote, `119-hr-8800` 19-amendment control byte-identical. **HO 552:** the gate collapsed "votes row not synced yet" (pending) and "synced, wrong question" (settled) to one no-link, but 551's own comment falsely claimed the absent case self-heals on re-walk — the queue predicate (`update_date > walked_at`) can't deliver it (a just-voted HAMDT already spent its `update_date` bump getting queued), so a decisive link dropped permanently; fixed by leaving an absent-row HAMDT UNSTAMPED to retry (`deferred++`, `continue` — the `fetchErrors` non-stamp precedent). **HO 553 STEP 0 proved the defer fires** — a scratch `voteId` perturbation gave `deferred=1` / unstamped / `remaining=1` pinned across 3 runs, and the retry completed on restore; nothing committed. **No theme-% change** — a correctness fix on the amendments pillar; combined `Lobbying / amendments / nominations` bar holds at 92. CSS additive under `.lob-exp-desc*` only — no `.lob-*`/`.mc-*`/`PAGE_SIZE`/rail retune. **CDP verification caught a real bug:** the handoff's char-threshold + bare-clamped-below-threshold would have *silently clipped* a below-threshold description, because `pre-wrap` makes the 4-line clamp count hard newlines as lines (column CDP-measured at 56 chars/line → 4 lines ≈ 224, under the 280 threshold); fixed by rendering below-threshold UNCLAMPED (`--open`, clip-proof), demoting the threshold to cosmetic. Monster (`cd77f510…`) prod-renders 27 clamp+button / 4 short `--open` = 31 activities; open = +330px bounded, not +6000. Clears the QUEUED line-clamp item. See roadmap "Also (HO 545)" + oddities. - ~~**Amendment roll-call votes, House half + the latent 500 (HO 532 walk · HO 533 fix)**~~ — **SHIPPED `3c4c72c` (House) · `85d6660` (fix)**, prod-verified on the cache-hit path. Completes the `recordedVotes` gap across both chambers (Senate was HO 530). **HO 532:** an `amendment_votes` link table (composite PK) + an `amendment_vote_walked_at` sentinel on `amendments` (sync-invisible, HO 459 pattern) + a partial `idx_amendments_hamdt` walk-queue index; `sync:amendment-votes --walk` fetches each HAMDT's `/actions`, constructs the deterministic `house-{congress}-{session}-{rollNumber}` vote_id, upserts the link, stamps the sentinel (re-walk keyed on `update_date > walked_at`); a bounded cron walk step in `/api/cron/amendments`. `getBillAmendmentVotes` widened to SAMDT+HAMDT with a new `amendment_votes → votes` join; render unchanged (chamber-agnostic). Backfill 267 HAMDT walked / 92 links / 90 amendments / 0 errors. **HO 533 — two fixes, one blocking:** `getBillAmendmentVotes` returned a `Map`, which `unstable_cache` round-trips to `{}` on a **cache hit**, so `BillAmendments` threw `votes.get is not a function` and 500'd every amendment-bearing `/bill/[id]` ~59min/hr **since HO 530** (hidden because every verify landed in the post-deploy cache-miss window); fixed → plain `Record`. Plus a zero-split guard in `VoteLine` (suppress a false `D 0-0 · R 0-0` when member_votes lag). A repo-wide audit confirmed it was the only Map/Set/Date-returning cached function. **No theme-% change** — linkage completion of the 519 gap + a correctness fix; combined bar holds at 92. See roadmap "Also (HO 532–533)"; oddities (2 process lessons). Docs: HO 534.
- ~~**Amendment roll-call votes, Senate half (HO 529 probe · HO 530 surface)**~~ — **probe `e0131cf`, surface SHIPPED `1f9f748`**, prod-verified. The HO 452 `recordedVotes` lead, built probe-first, and the probe **retired the backlog's `/actions`-walk premise** (the "a backlog line's mechanism is a claim" rule, HO 512/514). Findings: `votes.amendment_designation` is dead for amendments (nominations only, 0/431 join); **Senate joins FREE** via a `votes.question` regex (`On the Amendment S.Amdt. N …`) — 114 SAMDT, 100% on tracked bills, `member_votes` present, so query-only, zero backfill; **House needs the walk** (generic question, no number) but `/actions.recordedVotes.rollNumber` resolves 100% to existing `votes` rows, so it's a bounded *linkage* backfill, not a member-vote backfill. `getBillAmendmentVotes(billId)` (a separate cached query, `getBillAmendments` untouched) returns `Map<amendmentId, AmendmentVote[]>` (a list — 3 amendments carry a table+substantive pair); the `/bill/[id]` AMENDMENTS section shows the tally + D/R/I party split, and the display-only disposition dot becomes authoritative from `votes.result` where a vote exists. `votes.id` is a TEXT composite (`senate-119-1-3`). **No theme-% change** — the Senate half of the 519 `recordedVotes` gap; combined bar holds at 92 (House + per-member drill still banked → QUEUED). See roadmap "Also (HO 529–530)". Docs: HO 531.
- ~~**`/members` participation dotplot (HO 527)**~~ — SHIPPED `7fa729c`, verified live. The population cut of the participation arc — `IdeologyStrip`'s twin on the 119th missed-vote rate. **`getParticipationStrip()`** (sibling of `getIdeologyStrip`, NOT an extension of `getChamberParticipationContext`) returns every floored current member (the identical B1 population — `is_current=1`, `HAVING total >= PARTICIPATION_FLOOR`, `not_voting/total`) + an `is_delegate` flag; **`ParticipationStrip`** clones `IdeologyStrip` with the metric deltas (domain 0..CAP=30, 0.5pp Wilkinson bins, party-colored dots, chamber-split HSE/SEN median ticks in neutral amber, no empty-center annotation). Delegates carved from cloud+median via the territorial predicate (`chamber='house' AND state IN` the 6 territorial codes), count disclosed in the footer. Placed open below `IdeologyStrip`, rescoped by the chamber toggle only; CSS additive under `.part-strip-*`, `IdeologyStrip` byte-identical. STEP-0 finding: the carve-out is population-correctness, not outlier-trimming — the real high tail is a voting member (Wilson 29% > 4 of 6 delegates); only Radewagen 82% / Plaskett 50% are extreme (oddities). No bar move (enabling surface, the HO 425 precedent). Docs: HO 528.
- ~~**Career missed-vote rate on the member hub (B2, HO 525)**~~ — **SHIPPED `bf63b23`**, prod-verified. `member_career_votes` rollup (bioguide PK) built by manual **`sync:career-votes`** (npm), truncate-then-recompute off Voteview's per-Congress votes files streamed **94→119** both chambers (~356MB, flat ~130MB peak — the accumulator is 536 members × a few ints, so memory doesn't grow with range). The hub hero carries **`Missed · career · N Congresses`** beside B1's `Missed · 119th` (`getMemberCareerVotes`, a cached bioguide point-lookup; null → no stat). **cast_code rule (HO 524 correction):** cast = 1–8 (7/8 = Present, folded in), miss = 9, exclude = 0/absent — see oddities. **Current-only** via an `is_current=1` join (member_ideology retains 552 once-matched rows vs 536 current — oddities). Cross-checks passed: **Collins 0/10,062 since 1997** (perfect-attendance streak), **Grassley 1.09%/26C**, **Fetterman 13.1%**; delegates show structural inflation (own-rate kept, rank-only carve-out banked); **Graham (no icpsr) → graceful null**, verified on the hub. See roadmap "Also (HO 522–525)". Docs: HO 526.

The member's own 119th missed-rate was already on the hero; B1 added the **chamber median** beside it as context (`getChamberParticipationContext`, one cached all-member distribution, args-free — **no new table**). **Floor F=50** over the population for both the median and rank-eligibility (floored only 3 departed members, clips zero current). **Median shipped; the bare attendance rank was computed-then-dropped** — delegate-inflated (structural non-voting) and unused, the drop-unused-field call (the rank carve-out is banked below). - ~~**SC Senate seat change — deceased-senator wrong-incumbent on `/electoral` (HO 519 §5 finding → HO 520 fix)**~~ — **FIXED HO 520, zero code / zero commits.** The recurred **`sync:members`-staleness class** (HO 402 Gallagher CA-01 shape): a mid-Congress seat change the manual quarterly-plus sync hadn't picked up, surfaced by the HO 519 audit as `S-SC-2026` rendering a **deceased senator as the sitting incumbent**. **Ground truth (verified post-cutoff via governor.sc.gov — the HO 422 Kiley verify-before-fix discipline, NOT dismissed as bad yaml):** Sen. Lindsey Graham (`G000359`, R-SC, class 2) **died** (served 2003–2026); Gov. McMaster appointed **Darline Graham (`G000608`)** as caretaker until 2027-01-03. Upstream `legislators-current.yaml` already reflected it (dropped `G000359`, carries `G000608` as the sitting SC class-2 R). CBT was stale — `G000359` still `is_current=1`, `G000608` absent, `S-SC-2026.incumbent_bioguide_id=G000359` — fixed by the **sanctioned `sync:members` → `sync:crosswalk` → `backfill:races`** (the HO 412 `is_current=1`-filtered upsert). Re-dumps confirmed `G000608` added (`is_current=1`, class 2), `G000359` flipped (`is_current=0`), `S-SC-2026` incumbent moved to `G000608`; the **HO 410 audit re-ran fully green** (§1/§2/§4 all 0/0), and the live `/electoral` + `/race/S-SC-2026` now render Darline Graham (100 current senators intact). SC is class 2, so the HO 411 class-derived `next_election_year=2026` was **unaffected** (no `senate-special-elections.json` entry — SC is on-cycle, not an off-cycle special like OH/FL). **Orthogonal, not chased:** `S-SC-2026` carries a **pre-existing NULL `rating`** (surfaced during the fix — a separate low item, unrelated to the incumbent). The Aug-11 special-primary re-ingest is parked (see BANKED). See roadmap "Also (HO 519–520)". Docs: HO 521.
- ~~**Shared record box — member hub + `/bill/[id]` onto one tabbed shell (HO 509–510)**~~ — **SHIPPED** across `8538660` (HO 509 — the component + the member hub) and `d22fe66` (HO 510 — `/bill/[id]`). New `components/RecordTabs.tsx`, a `"use client"` island on the `ActivityTabs` contract (holds only tab state, takes pre-rendered `ReactNode`s, all panels mount with `hidden`, `tabs.length === 0 → null`). **Member hub:** hero → one bordered card in three bands (identity + five-figure stat run · `MemberIdeology` promoted out of Voting record · the metabox unrolled sideways with `data-cells` empty-collapse), six sections → one box (Bills · Votes · Committees · Amendments · Press · Trades + conditional Scorecard). **`/bill/[id]`:** four sections → the same box (Committees · Hearings · Amendments · Lobbying, fixed process order), one additive `initialKey` prop opening the heaviest tab without the strip reordering. **Four selectors retired** (`SectionShell`, `.bdp-pair`, `.bdp-amend-scroll`, `.bdp-out`); **two member components deleted** (`MemberAffiliations`, `MemberStats` — both grep-confirmed single-consumer); **`MemberHeader` lost its election chip + `rating` prop + local `ratingColor()`** (the "keep in sync with `RatingChip`" duplicate discharged). **The two empty-state policies are opposite and both correct** — member tabs dim at zero (a fact about a person), bill tabs are omitted at zero (a bill with none of the four gets no box), see oddities. **`key={entity}`** on both call sites, insurance against a same-instance reconcile stranding the lazy-`useState` tab index (oddities). Hero untouched, feed expand panel byte-identical (the HO 507 containment rule held). **This extends the HO 328 vocabulary arc** the 507 tombstone called complete: the three browse surfaces converged on `.mc-*`, the two detail pages now converge on a second shared shell. Roadmap: the "Also (HO 509–510)" block. Docs: HO 511.
- ~~**`/bill/[id]` page redesign**~~ — **SHIPPED HO 507 (`eaf79a4`).** The detail page rebuilt as the feed expand panel's **big sibling**: a bordered hero reusing the shared `BillStageBar` + `.bxp-body` grid (summary prose + RELATED NEWS via `getNewsForBill(bill.id, 5)`, the one added read) + the boxed `.bxp-metabox`, over member-hub section shells — COMMITTEES | HEARINGS `.bdp-pair` (lone survivor full-width), AMENDMENTS with a 340px scroll bound, LOBBYING. **Four condensation moves** (the HO 492 fold-into-header precedent): standalone LATEST ACTION collapsed into the metabox, amendment + lobbying stat lines pulled into their shell headers, footer squeezed to one row. **Containment:** zero edits to the shared `.bxp-*`/`.v2f-bar` rules; every deviation additive under a new `.bdp` scope — feed expand panel verified **byte-identical**. **`TopicTags` deleted** (its last consumer), closing the HO 316 holdout so the **B6 chip family — topic 316 · ID 466 · stage 467 · party 468 — is now genuinely complete**, not "complete except one page"; `TopicChips` carries the metabox topics. `getBillById` gained three plain `bills` columns (`sponsor_bioguide_id` / `cosponsor_count` / `stage_changed_at`) — the optional-`FeedBill`-field-vs-SELECT gap, see oddities → HO 508. Display-only sponsor strip so the colored `PartyTag` isn't double-printed (the query keeps the embedded form). Roadmap: the HO 507 `Also` block. Docs: HO 508.
- ~~**Playwright smoke-crawler CI wiring → Phase 2 Playwright-in-CI**~~ — **CLOSED HO 504 by explicit tombstone with a REPLACED criterion**, not by meeting the original one (the convention's second door — "an item leaves OPEN LOOPS only by meeting its close-criterion or being explicitly tombstoned"). Opened Jun 30 (HO 379), carried through five HOs. **Original criterion:** the e2e job runs green in CI against a **real DB** with the interaction hardenings. **Replaced with:** an unattended **prod** crawl (`.github/workflows/e2e-prod.yml`, HO 504) on `deployment_status` + daily schedule + `workflow_dispatch`, **no fixture, no secrets**, off the per-commit path. **Why:** the HO 503 probe found the specs guard with `test.skip`-on-empty, so an under-seeded fixture **passes while exercising nothing** — false-green, strictly worse than the red-blindness it was meant to cure (oddities) — and pre-merge protection buys minutes in a single-committer flow that already diff-gates + prod-verifies every change, at the cost of a hundreds-of-row artifact maintained forever against a 132-statement schema. The bucket count (73 tests, A 41 · B 28 · C 2 · D 2 · E 0, 95% nominally servable) said build it; the failure-mode analysis said stop, and it was right. **Fallback considered and NOT taken** (recorded so it isn't re-litigated): a few-dozen-row fixture backing **only** the (A) route crawl tagged `@ci`, never the guarded interaction tests — available if pre-merge #418 protection is ever explicitly wanted. **The probe's one open question — does every (A) route 200 on a migrated-but-empty DB, or 500 on a `rows[0]` access — is MOOT under this verdict** (no fixture is built), not a dangling measurement someone should go take. Hardenings: #2 (console allowlist) + #3 (bill selector) shipped in `fc42872`; #1 (freeze-the-marquee) retired as moot-under-BotID. One surviving thread — the `schedule` first-fire — stays in OPEN LOOPS. Detail: HO 503 report + HO 504.
- ~~**`queryEnactedThisWeek` cold-stall → enacted-helper hint (HO 481)**~~ — SHIPPED `9c19557`. The 5th and last known sibling of the HO 405/407/480 OR-lure, flagged-not-touched by the 480 sweep because it's a shared HO 232 helper (the ENACTED THIS WEEK banner + the week-over-week delta both consume it). Hint-first, no migration: `INDEXED BY idx_bills_enacted` on both `queryEnactedThisWeek` and `queryEnactedPriorWeekCount` — the partial index (`WHERE stage='enacted'`) qualifies because the query carries `AND stage='enacted'`, collapsing `MULTI-INDEX OR` over `idx_bills_dash_stage` + temp-btree → `SCAN bills USING INDEX idx_bills_enacted` (cold 617ms / 106ms, well under the 10s `boundedFetch` wall; identical rows/count vs unhinted). Low-severity by construction (the `stage='enacted'` seek keeps it off the full corpus, so it never tipped the wall — closed for hygiene, to shut the known instances of the class). **Corrected premise:** HO 335's assumption that this rode `idx_bills_enacted` cleanly was wrong — that index keys `(congress, latest_action_date)`, has no `stage_changed_at`, so a residual in-memory sort remains (trivial at the enacted row count). See roadmap "Also (HO 481)". Docs: HO 481.
- ~~**`/api/sync` trailing-step timeout → lead-query hint sweep (HO 479 probe / HO 480 fix)**~~ — SHIPPED `b440b82`. **The overrun was mis-diagnosed by its own symptom:** 32 of 40 ticks timed out at the 55s cap, but the trailing step wasn't slow-Gemini (HO 479's hypothesis) — it was the HO 405/407 **cold-DB-stall**. `gatherLeadData`'s transitions query, OR-lured (`is_ceremonial=0 OR IS NULL`) into a `MULTI-INDEX OR` over `idx_bills_is_ceremonial` (~the whole 16.8k corpus) + a temp-btree sort, **ignored the perfect `idx_bills_stage_changed_at`** and cold-stalled past the 10s `boundedFetch` — retrying to 10+10s and *failing* the (non-fatal) lead step, so the dashboard lead had been **silently going stale on every real-delta tick**, worse than the visible timeout implied. HO 479 (read-only probe `scripts/diagnostic/sync-trailing-cost-479.ts`) pinned it; HO 480 swept `gatherLeadData` — `INDEXED BY` on `transRs`/`introRs`/`topicRs`, the 407 remedy applied to the sibling function 407 missed — collapsing lead from the 14–20s stall band to ~1–3s cold, wall now ~33–40s under the 55s cap. **Deferred (own HO):** timeout rows carry no per-step timings — `wrapCronRoute` discards the handler payload on the `Promise.race` reject — a fleet-wide contract change, not needed once the route stops timing out. The 4th lead read (`queryEnactedThisWeek`) mis-plans too → new OPEN LOOP (closed HO 481). See roadmap "Also (HO 480)". Docs: HO 480.
- ~~**Markets watchdog → cron-health watchdog + `/api/health` (HO 477)**~~ — SHIPPED `92baba0`, prod-verified (200 / `healthy:true` / 14 routes). Generalized from markets-only to a fleet-wide check: `lib/cron-health.ts` (`CRON_ROUTES` registry + `computeCronHealth`) feeds a new `/api/health` GET (200/503) + the refreshed HO 135 diagnostic. **Liveness = a `cron_runs` row in the route's window** (success OR timeout — timeout is HO 139's fired-and-executed, not a stop) + error/orphaned as the hard-fail. **Markets keyed on `market_ticks` freshness** (the literal "no `market_ticks` write in N hours" this loop asked for), unhealthy iff ticks > ~9h stale OR error/orphaned — the permanent POLY-SHUTDOWN chronicErr is ignored. Endpoint is unauthed, `force-dynamic`/`no-store`, not `wrapCronRoute`-wrapped, 503s on a check failure. **The push layer is out-of-repo:** point an uptime monitor (UptimeRobot / cron-job.org) at `/api/health`, alert on non-200 — the only thing that catches a silently-stopped cron (Vercel can't; no invocation to error on). Docs reconciled here (HO 478): roadmap "Also (HO 477)" (pointer → 477), backlog (this tombstone + the two edits below), oddities ×2, SKILL (`/api/health` note). Doc sweep: HO 478.
- ~~**Intraday-reliable markets upgrade → markets/kalshi cron migration to Vercel Pro (HO 474 recon / HO 475 cutover)**~~ — SHIPPED across `f3fb87a` (arm) · `945486f` (retire), live SHA `945486f`; all three confirmed as distinct Vercel-cron GETs (fmp 17:00 / kalshi 18:15 / bare 20:00). The loop wanted the 30-min refresh off GitHub Actions onto a "free hosted cron"; the answer is **Vercel Pro's native cron** (the Hobby daily cap that forced GitHub is gone since HO 433). markets-tick → `/api/cron/markets?source=fmp` `0,30 13-21 * * 1-5`; markets-daily → the repurposed `/api/cron/markets` bare `0 */4` (was the dormant HO 314 `30 21` floor); kalshi-tick → `/api/cron/kalshi` `15 */2`; three workflows deleted. Overlap benign (append / upsert), so a clean single cutover. Deferred: FRED once-daily gate (bloat non-issue). Banked: markets-watchdog + ~200-row dead-symbol prune. Docs reconciled here (HO 476): roadmap "Also (HO 475)" (pointer → 475), backlog (this tombstone + the two edits below), oddities ×2, SKILL (cron-topology rewrite). Doc sweep: HO 476.
