Prod-verified `ssfr00` (201/100) · `ssju00` (187/118) · `hsju00` (omitted). **Two wrong premises the closed item carried, corrected** (diagnose-before-scoping, HO 420/422 precedent): (a) the "10% civilian referral was a confound" is **resolved** — 458 re-measured **98% presence / 100% resolution** on the correct per-part record; (b) the item's "the referral is in the action text; parse it" was **wrong** — referral is a clean **structured** `committees` sub-resource on the per-part detail (resolving 100% against tracked committees, **no text-parse**). `nominee_count` was scoped **OUT** (military-only, no consumer) and moved to the BANKED military-view item. - ~~**Amendments status/disposition model**~~ — **CLOSED NO-GO HO 452** (probe `67ed564`). The persisted-enum model would have walked each amendment's `/actions` sub-resource to classify the silent 91% the top-level `latest_action_text` misses, at ~2× the backfill (~6,800 → ~13,600 requests). The probe (`scripts/diagnostic/amendments-actions-probe-452.ts`, stratified n=155, 0 errors) found **0% recovery**: no-top-level-action == genuinely filed-never-acted — both the general silent stratum and the decisive `119-sconres-7` vote-a-rama stratum returned 100% submit/propose-only `/actions`, empty residual (a real absence, not a classifier miss). A vote-a-rama files ~1,131 and votes on ~24; the ~24 already carry a top-level action. - ~~**Ideology diagnostic — `party_code` vs caucus, not registration (HO 430)**~~ — SHIPPED `d061284`, the second of the two ideology-arc tail cleanups. `scripts/diagnostic/ideology-coverage-419.ts` compared Voteview `party_code`→letter against `members.party` by string equality, structurally false-positiving on independents who caucus with a major party (DW-NOMINATE `party_code` follows **caucus**, `members.party` follows **registration**). - ~~**Summarize cron doom loop — bounded queue read + concurrency (HO 405 probe / HO 406 fix)**~~ — SHIPPED `82fb6bc`, live on prod, drained end-to-end. **HO 405** diagnosed two co-occurring causes; **HO 406** fixed both. **Mode 1 (aborts):** HO 383 dropped the queue read's SQL LIMIT to fetch the full null set for the app-side stage-priority sort; at 1,374 nulls it became `SCAN bills USING INDEX idx_bills_update_date` over all 16,623 rows and tipped the 10s `boundedFetch` (the 07-02/06-29 55s soft-timeouts + the 06-30 20s error). Fixed with a partial index `idx_bills_summarize_queue ON bills(update_date DESC) WHERE summary IS NULL`, forced `INDEXED BY` — the statless planner mis-plans into a `MULTI-INDEX OR` on `idx_bills_is_ceremonial` unhinted (verified). Read → `SCAN … idx_bills_summarize_queue`: **5.07s cold** at full backlog, **48ms** drained (both under the 10s wall). The app-side `computeStage` priority sort is preserved — stored `stage` is stale by design (HO 383), so an SQL stage sort can't replace it; the handoff's "push priority into SQL with LIMIT" option was rejected for this reason. **Mode 3 (throughput):** serial ~1.9s/bill cleared only ~23/tick vs ~30/day inflow → a bounded **C=5** worker pool in `lib/summarize-runner.ts` (per-bill 15s AbortController unchanged, so up to C in flight at the 45s deadline still tail out ≤15s — the 45+15=60s ceiling holds), and the cron per-tick cap **50→200** so the budget is the wall. One real prod tick: **80** summarized, 0 fail/timeout, `budgetStopped`, 49.6s. **Drain:** one unbounded `npm run summarize` cleared **1,374 → 7** (the 7 were 15s per-bill timeouts, deferred 24h, retry next tick). **Two gotchas → oddities:** (1) building the partial index over 16k rows itself tipped the 10s `boundedFetch`, so `npm run migrate` aborted it — built once out-of-band with a long-timeout libsql client, `CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS` no-ops thereafter. (2) the ceremonial filter added to the queue is a verified no-op (`is_ceremonial` is set only at summarize time; sync resets it to NULL), kept as harmless feed-alignment, NOT the inflow reduction the brief assumed. Non-goals held: no schedule change, no Pro upgrade, no model/prompt change. Docs reconciled here (HO 406): backlog (this tombstone + the closed OPEN LOOP, Foundation-96-no-longer-fused note) + oddities ×2. Doc/code: HO 406.

- ~~**Weekly-report cron reliability (HO 284 probe + 285 catch-up)**~~ — SHIPPED 2026-06-20. *(Was the HO 242 first-cron-write OPEN LOOP.)* The weekly Monday report cron chronically dropped a week on any transient hiccup — a single-shot gen with no retry. **284** corrected the premise: the "20s LLM abort" (#287) was `boundedFetch`'s 10s+10s Turso double-timeout (a DB cold-stall in `gatherReportData`), not the LLM (the report's Gemini call has no abortSignal); three independent drop modes (DB cold-stall, Gemini 503, slow-Gemini >55s). **285** added a daily catch-up in `/api/sync` (`runReportCatchup`) that regenerates the most-recent missing of the last 4 completed weeks, one per run, idempotent — "retried daily until it lands," defeating all three. - ~~**Gated-aggregate mis-plan 500s (was "cold-start 500" BLOCKER) — CLEARED via HO 277–279**~~ — DONE 2026-06-19. The `/dashboard-v2` cold-start 500 wasn't a cold-start problem at all — it was a whole CLASS of summary-gated fat-`bills` COUNT/aggregates the statless Turso planner mis-plans onto `idx_bills_is_ceremonial` (a MULTI-INDEX OR over ~every row), slow even warm + nondeterministic (112ms↔18s+). It surfaced as live 10s-abort 500s on **`/members`** (HO 277, digest 4101894172), **`/dashboard-v2`** (HO 278, the swap blocker), and **`/bills`** (HO 279). Fixed with additive covering/partial indexes + gated `INDEXED BY` hints (the planner refuses them unhinted): `idx_bills_summary_feed` (shared: getFeedStats / getCorpusStats(true) / getFeedBills COUNT), `idx_bills_summary_stage`, `idx_bills_summary_topics`, `idx_bills_sponsor_agg`. **getFeedStats was app-wide** (masthead, every HeaderBar page) — fixing it protected every inner page. Cold-verified: `/members` 0.885s, `/dashboard-v2` 5× cold 200, `/bills` 0.705s first-hit-after-idle. 9 commits (`859466a`…`b423329`). The v2→`/` swap is unblocked (→ QUEUED). Residual same-class siblings → WATCH below. Doc sweep: HO 280.

- ~~**Homepage `/` cold-start 500**~~ — FIXED 2026-06-14/15 (HO 241; commits `d7c2435` forced indexes + `3880c59` prewarm revert). First `/` hit after Turso idle-evicted the `bills` page cache took ~18–20s and tripped the 10s `DB_REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS` abort (×2 retry = 20s 500); the recompute couldn't populate the Data Cache before aborting, so it never self-healed. *Root cause:* the ~63MB `bills` table cold-scanned by ~6 homepage queries, and the **statless planner** (ANALYZE blocked on Turso, like VACUUM) grabbed `idx_bills_is_ceremonial` for the `(is_ceremonial=0 OR NULL)` OR — ~13–20s **even warm**. *Fix:* 2 covering indexes (`idx_bills_dash_stage`, `idx_bills_dash_topics`) for the aggregates + `INDEXED BY` hints on 5 feed queries forcing the existing date/news indexes. Cold `/` 500@20s → 200@~2s, **self-recovering via populate-on-read** (the cron prewarm built mid-investigation was reverted — dead problem once the queries are fast). Detour tombstoned: the prewarm + `revalidate:false` approach (it 504'd the sync route stacking cold queries behind the unbounded Gemini lead). See HO 241 ABANDONED banner + `SKILL.md` forced-index note.
